BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK                                             業務回顧及展望

In 2023, the economy in China gradually returned to a recovery path     2023 年 ， 中 國 經 濟 隨 著 當 局 優 化 疫 情 防
as the government relaxed the pandemic prevention and control           控措施而逐漸恢復，惟造紙行業下游需求
measures. Although the paper manufacturing industry as a whole          偏弱，加上新增產能集中釋放，造紙業整
performed below expectations owing to the combined effect of a          體表現低於預期，但下半年已逐漸回暖。
weak downstream demand and a surge in newly added production            另一方面，本集團已積極推動垂直業務模
capacity, the market recovery is gathering pace during the second       式，新增製漿產能陸續投產，本集團下半
half of the year. On the other hand, the Group has been actively        年業績明顯比上半年改善。
pursuing a vertical business model and its newly added pulp-making
capacity has also gradually been put into operation. As a result, the
Group’s performance in the second half of the year showed a
significant improvement compared to the first half.


For 2023, the Group recorded total revenue of HK$24.9 billion,          本 集 團 2023 年 總 收 入 比 去 年 下 跌 14.5%
representing a decrease of 14.5% from that of the previous year,        至 249 億 港 元 ， 年 度 盈 利 較 去 年 下 調
while the profit for the year decreased by 12.4% to HK$1.2 billion as   12.4%至12億港元。年內每股盈利24.39
compared with that for the previous year. The earnings per share for    港仙（2022年：27.46港仙）。

INDUSTRY AND BUSINESS REVIEW                                              行業及業務回顧

During the year under review, as the PRC government implemented           回 顧 年 內 ， 內 地 實 施 對 多 種 紙 品 進 口「 零
the “zero tariff” on the import of various paper products, and the        關 稅 」， 疊 加 行 業 新 增 產 能 陸 續 投 產 ， 造
industry’s newly-added production capacity was progressively put          紙企業普遍下調價格迎戰。另一方面，市
into operation, paper manufacturing companies generally adjusted          場需求於上半年表現疲軟，行業處於去庫
their prices downward in the face of the competition. On the other        存週期，營運效益承壓；及至下半年傳統
hand, weak market demand and the industry’s destocking cycle in           消費旺季，紙品需求逐漸回暖，紙價連番
the first half of the year put pressure on operating efficiency. During   上升，行業盈利得以改善。
the second half of the year, the traditional peak season saw a
rebound in demand for paper products, and the profitability of the
entire industry improved in line with the gradual increase in paper
prices.


In the face of changes in market supply and demand and policy             面對市場供需及政策變化，本集團積極推
adjustments, the Group actively consolidated its upstream resources       動產業鏈向上游延伸，發展涵蓋製漿及廢
to develop a vertical business model covering pulp-making and waste       紙回收的垂直業務模式，以確保原材料供
paper recycling, with a view to ensuring raw material supply and          應，控制成本以改善盈利。回顧年內，本
controlling its costs so as to improve profitability. During the year     集團位於重慶、江西和廣西的新增木漿生
under review, the Group’s new pulp production lines in Chongqing,         產線已陸續投產，新增年產能95萬噸，木
Jiangxi and Guangxi commenced operation in succession, with an            漿總產能共120萬噸。
additional annual production capacity of 950,000 tons and a total
pulp production capacity of 1.2 million tons.


The overseas production bases that the Group’s previously established     本集團此前佈局的海外生產基地於回顧年
have demonstrated their cost advantages and generated substantial         內發揮成本優勢，提供可觀盈利。為進一

BUSINESS PROSPECTS                                                       業務前景

Packaging paper remains the Group’s core business. In response to        包裝紙業務為本集團的主要核心業務。響
the carbon emission reduction target, the State Council successively     應 減 碳 目 標 ， 國 務 院 先 後 發 布《 關 於 進 一
promulgated the policies of the Notice on Further Strengthening the      步加強商品過度包裝治理的通知》及《國務
Control of Excessive Packaging of Commodities (《關於進一步加強商                 院 辦 公 廳 關 於 印 發「 十 四 五 」現 代 物 流 發
品過度包裝治理的通知》) and the Circular of the General Office of                   展規劃的通知》等政策，加強管控塑料包
the State Council on Printing and Issuing the Development Plan for       裝，為紙質包裝提供替代空間，有利本集
Modern Logistics during the 14th Five-Year Plan Period (《國務院辦            團包裝紙業務。隨著內地加大經濟支持力
公廳關於印發「十四五」現代物流發展規劃的通知》) t o t i g h t e n                               度，內需消費意慾預期將進一步改善，包
control on plastic packaging and promote the use of paper packaging      裝紙等主要紙品的需求亦逐漸回升。
as a substitute, which will benefit the Group’s packaging paper
business. As the PRC government has placed greater emphasis on
supporting economic recovery, domestic consumption sentiment is
expected to improve, and demand for key paper products such as
packaging paper will also gradually pick up.


Regarding the overseas markets, the Group will leverage on the           海外市場方面，本集團將善用馬來西亞及
manufacturing facilities in Malaysia and Vietnam and local resources     越南的生產設施，配合當地資源，優化營

Anti-bribery, Anti-fraud and Anti-money Laundering                            防止賄賂、欺詐及洗黑錢
The Group attaches great importance to integrity supervision, and is          本集團高度重視廉潔監管，致力防範貪
committed to preventing corruption, bribery, fraud and other                  腐、賄賂、欺詐等違法和違反商業道德的
behaviors that are illegal and against commercial ethics, to make sure        行為，確保營運全程遵守所有防止商業罪
we have always observed laws and regulations on prevention of                 案 有 關 的 法 例 法 規 ， 包 括 及 不 限 於《 中 華
commercial crimes in our daily operations, including but not limited          人 民 共 和 國 刑 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反
to the “Criminal Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the “Anti-           洗 錢 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反 不 正 當 競
Money Laundering Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the                  爭法》等。法規要求企業需遵循平等、公
“Anti-Unfair Competition Law of the People’s Republic of China”               平、誠信等原則，依法而行，堅守商業道
and so forth. Enterprises are required under laws and regulations to          德，不得以盜竊、賄賂、欺詐、脅迫或者
abide by the principles of fairness, equality and integrity, to act in a      以其他不正當手段經營。
lawful manner, to adhere to commercial ethics, and not to operate
business by means of theft, bribery, deception, compulsion or other
improper means.


The Group believes that to achieve anti-corruption, it relies the             本集團相信實現反貪污有賴多方內外合
cooperation of both internal and external parties within the                  作。因此，所有員工亦需要遵守本集團編
organization. Therefore, all employees are required to abide by the           製的《商業道德規範》及《反貪污政策》要求
“Standards for Commercial Ethics” and “Anti-Corruption Policy”                員工以專業合規、平等公正、誠信正直的
complied by the Group, which restricts employees to serve the                 行為服務公司和客戶。本集團的員工守則
Company and customers with professional compliance, equality,                 中亦明文規定了個人及專業操守以期嚴格
fairness, and integrity. The Worker’s Manual of the Group also clearly        制約、防範交易過程出現任何特殊待遇，
stipulates code of personal and professional conduct for their                包括但不限於回扣、昂貴禮品、奢侈款待
stringent compliance, pursuant to which employees are not allowed             等，以免影響其客觀專業的判斷。同時，
to accept any special privilege during transactions, including but not        本 集 團 員 工 在 入 職 時 均 須 簽 署《 廉 潔 從 業
limited to rebates, expensive gifts, luxury hospitality, etc., so as not to   承 諾 書 》， 承 諾 不 可 以 利 用 工 作 職 權 上 的
affect their objectivity and professional judgment. In addition, all          便利為自己索取任何好處，不得為牟取自
employees of the Group are required to sign “Undertaking to                   身利益而損害公司權益。
Integrity” upon onboarding, undertaking that he/she will not use his/
her position to seek for unfair benefits nor gain personal profits to
the detriment of the Company.

Prohibition of Child and Forced Labour                                     防止童工或強制勞工
The Group recruits qualified workers in adherence to the principles of     本集團按照公平、公開、自願的原則招聘
fairness, openness and free will. Every subsidiary of the Group            錄 用 合 資 格 的 員 工 。 各 個 子 公 司 根 據《 中
entered into legal employment contract with employees in                   華人民共和國勞動合同法》或當地適用勞
accordance with the “Labour Contract Law of the People’s Republic          工法與員工簽訂合法的僱傭合同，無強制
of China” or applicable local labour laws and there was no forced          勞 工 行 為 。 同 時 ， 本 集 團 嚴 格 按 照《 中 華
labour. In the meantime, the Group only recruits people who have           人民共和國勞動法》及其他適用的當地勞
attained the age of 16 in strict compliance with the “Labour Law of        工法要求，只招聘錄用已年滿16周歲的人
the People’s Republic of China” and other applicable local labour          士 。 本 集 團 內 部 設 立《 未 成 年 工 使 用 及 禁
laws and regulations. The Group formulated its own “Management             止使用童工管理規定》防止童工或不法勞
Rules for the Use of Minors as Labourers and Prohibition against the       工，保障未成年人的合法權益。人力資源
Use of Child Labour” to prevent the use of child or illegal labour and     部會在招聘流程中核對應聘者的個人資料
to protect the legitimate rights and interest of the minors. During the    並進行背景調查，本集團會保留任何調查
recruitment process, the human resources department would check            結果異常之個案的錄用登記及核查材料，

investigation. If such investigation generated any irregular result, the   冊》中鼓勵員工舉報使用他人身份證明文
Group would retain the record and cease the application process            件的情況，從多方面達成管控目標。
accordingly. In the “Worker’s Manual“, the Group also encourages

understand to protect employees’ rights and interests. At the same         程中向員工或其代理商收取任何形式的費

Being well aware that environmental protection is an important basis    本集團深明環境保護是可持續發展的重要
for its development, the Group has adopted a number of                  基礎，因此採取多項改進措施及監控方法

our environmental responsibilities. The Group has established various   的各項環保政策與措施包括：
environmental protection policies and measures including:

Setting Up Environmental Team
成立環保小組

Led by our Chief Executive Officer, the environmental team comprises members having the relevant expertise,
including senior flue gas treatment experts, sewage treatment technicians, environmental monitoring professionals
and environmental protection technicians so as to ensure effective implementation of the following measures.
由首席執行官親自帶領，由合資格專才組成，包括高級煙氣治理師、污水處理人員、環境監測人員、環境保護專業
技術人員等，保證下列相關環保措施得以有效執行。

Duties of Environmental Team
環保小組職責

1.   Holding internal control meetings regularly to review environmental issues in the plants, to update themselves
     with applicable environmental laws and regulations and to give advice on any improvements;
2.   On an ongoing basis, the team engages themselves in environmental protection measures to be taken in
     production processes, including those employed to lower water consumption, treat sewage water and air
     emissions more efficiently, save energy and monitoring emissions to ensure continued compliance with high
     environmental protection standards.
1.   定期召開內部監控會議，旨在審閱有關廠房內的環保事宜，通知各成員環保法律及規定的更新條文，並為存
     在改善空間的環節提供適當建議；
2.   不斷完善生產過程中的環保措施，包括減少耗水量、應用更有效的廢水處理技術、節省能源耗用量、採用更
     環保的廢氣處理技術及排放監控等，確保可持續地達到高環保標準。


Environmental Policies and Measures
環保政策與措施

✓    set up policies for emissions treatment to provide clear targets and state the responsibilities and division of
     labour for environmental protection measures adopted by the Group;
✓    formulate guidelines for energy-saving and reduce emission at the source;
✓    as part of the various efforts to make us one of the companies which best complies with environmental
     protection principles in the world, our different plants have employed technologies with high efficiency to
     reduce emission and keep our emission below the latest emission standards laid down by the government as
     well as internationally applicable environmental management standards.
✓    設立排放物處理辦法，為本集團採納之環保措施提供明確的目標和職責分工；
✓    訂立節約能源指引，從源頭減排；
✓    各廠房利用高效率技術降低排放，保持低於政府最新排放標準的排放水準，對標國際環境管理標準，並努力
     成為全球最符合環保原則的公司之一。

                              Case Study: Environmentally friendly Thermal Power Station
                                               個案研究：環保熱電站

Environmentally friendly circulating fluidised bed boilers used in our
thermal power stations can achieve efficient combustion of all
kinds of coal. As the combustion temperature in this kind of boilers
is lower than that in pulverised coal boilers, less nitrogen oxides
would be generated.
本集團的熱電站一致採用環保型的循環流化床鍋爐，可以有效
焚燒各煤種，該種鍋爐燃燒溫度低於煤粉爐，產生的氮氧化物
排放較少。


In each coal-fired power station, the most advanced air emissions
treatment facilities have been installed, including the employment
of highly efficient bag filters to reduce ashes, SCR/SNCR de-
nitration systems to eliminate nitrogen oxides, and limestone-
gypsum flue gas desulfurisation system to curb sulphur dioxide gas.
We have successfully reduced smoke and sulphur by more than
99.9% and 90% respectively.
每個燃煤發電廠均配備並使用最先進的廢氣處理設施，包括高
效布袋除塵器去除煙塵；SCR/SNCR脫硝法去除氮氧化物；石
灰石膏脫硫法去除二氧化硫等。目前煙塵去除率已達99.9%以
上，脫硫率達90%以上。                                                                  Circulating Fluidised Bed Boilers
                                                                                     循環流化床鍋爐
                                                                                                                   3
Our effective wet desulfurisation facility further reduced sulphur dioxide emissions to much less than 400 mg/m as
required by the “Emission Standard of Air Pollutants for Thermal Power Plants” (GB13223-2011) in China. The Group
has monitored the quality of air emissions on a real-time basis by using online detection equipment to ensure
outperforming the relevant national standard.
此外，我們高效的濕法脫硫措施，進一步把二氧化硫排放量降至遠低於國家GB13223-2011《火電廠大氣污染
物排放標準》要求的每立方米400毫克標準。本集團通過在線監察設備實時監測排放廢氣的質量，確保排放高
於國家規定的標準。


Among which, the combustion system of No. 1-4 circulating fluidised bed boilers of Guangdong Lee & Man were
optimized and reformed by adopting the LNB technology to reduce the raw emission of nitrogen oxides from boiler
                         3
flue gas to 110-130mg/Nm , so as to lay a solid foundation for the realization of ultra-low emission of nitrogen oxides
from boiler flue gas.
其中廣東理文的1-4號循環流體化床鍋爐進行燃燒系統優化改造，採用LNB技術，降低鍋爐煙氣氮氧化物原始
排放至110-130mg/Nm 3 ，為鍋爐煙氣氮氧化物實施超低 排放打好基礎。


No. 1-2 boilers adopted the PNCR+SNCR combined denitration technology, which effectively improved
the denitration efficiency and achieved the ultra-low emission standard of nitrogen oxides from boiler
flue gas of ≤50mg/Nm 3 .
1-2號鍋爐更採用PNCR+SNCR合併脫硝技術，有效提升脫硝效率，達到鍋爐菸氣氮氧化物超低排放標準，
≤50mg/Nm 3 。

Wastes                                                                      廢棄物
The wastes generated in our operations were mainly non-hazardous            本集團營運所產生的廢物主要為造紙過程
ones produced in the course of paper-making, together with a small          中所產生的無害廢棄物，亦有少量日常行
quantity of wastes due to routine office work. In order to reduce and       政辦公所產生的廢棄物。針對造紙過程中
dispose of waste produced in the paper-making process, the Group            所產生的廢棄物的減少和處理，本集團已
has established the “Guidelines for Waste Management” to classify           設立《廢棄物管理指導書》對本集團所有活
wastes of all activities and clarify the workflow of processing. Policies   動產生的廢棄物進行分類，明確其處理操
also provide for guidelines for the storage, transport and disposal of      作流程。政策亦為廢棄物的存放、運輸及
wastes and require them to be recorded to ensure that treatment             處置提供指引並要求記錄在案，確保處理
process is in compliance with rules and is safe.                            過程安全合規。

The Group has principally disposed of wastes generated from its             本集團主要通過以下三種方式處理廢棄
paper-making operations in three ways:                                      物：

•     Sewage treatment facilities in plants will filter sewage              •   工廠的污水處理設施過濾造紙過程
      generated from the paper-making process. This process would               中所產生的污水，平均每月會產生
      generate an average of over 10,000 tonnes of sludge. It is then           過萬噸污泥。污泥經處理及深度脫
      burnt in boilers after treatment and deep drying. The energy              水後被送至鍋爐焚燒，通過迴圈流
      generated by burning the waste in a circulating fluidised bed             化床鍋爐焚燒的廢物所產生的能量
      boilers could be reused in the paper-making process and                   用於造紙生產，不但不會造成二次
      therefore, not only secondary pollution would be avoided but              污染，還節省了能源消耗；
      also saving energy;

•     The Group used lots of waste paper as a raw material in its           •   本集團在造紙生產過程中使用大量
      paper-making operations, paper-making residue was left                    廢紙作為原料，廢紙經提取有用的
      behind after extraction of useful fibres for the paper-making             造紙纖維後剩下造紙廢渣。本集團
      process. As the residue contained different impurities, the               把造紙廢渣引進洗水線提取有價值
      residue was carried to a washing line where reusable metal                雜質，如鐵絲及塑膠等，並將此等
      wires and plastics in the residue were collected and sold to              物料出售予回收商進行回收再造；
      recyclers;

•     Established “Directory of Hazardous Wastes”, so as to                 •   編 製《 危 險 廢 棄 物 匯 總 表 》， 明 確 分
      segregate hazardous wastes before processing or disposal of               類危險廢棄物，繼而處理或出售可

              Case Study: Guangdong Lee & Man – Energy Comprehensive Utilization of Residue Project10
                                     個案研究：廣東理文－廢渣能源綜合利用項目10


The project builds a residue boiler – turbine generator set
that uses residue, waste plastic, sludge and biogas
generated from the paper production line as fuel to
generate steam and electricity for the boiler fuel; it can
reduce 340,000 tonnes of industrial solid waste, generate
108 million kWh of electricity and 550,000 tonnes of
steam in a year, which is equivalent to a reduction of
85,700 tonnes of raw coal and 156,000 tonnes of carbon
dioxide, with obvious environmental and economic
benefits.
工 程 建 置 廢 渣鍋 爐 － 汽 輪 發 電 機 組 ， 以 造 紙生產線產
生 的 廢 渣 、 廢塑 膠 、 污 泥 及 沼 氣 為 燃 料 ， 為鍋爐燃料
產 生 蒸 汽 和 電 力 ； 一 年 可 減 少 34 萬 噸 工 業 固 體 廢 棄
物，發電量1.08億度，產生蒸汽55萬噸；相當減少原
煤8.57萬噸，減少二氧化碳15.6萬噸，環保及經濟效
益明顯。




10    The project has an investment of RMB320 million and is currently in the   10
                                                                                     項 目 投 入 人 民 幣 3.2 億 元 ， 目 前 在 調 試 階 段 當
      commission stage.                                                              中。

Energy Consumption                                                        能源耗量
The logistics and production processes in our daily operations have       本集團日常營運中的運輸及生產過程都需
involved the use of great amounts of energy. Diesel and coal have         要大量能源供相關機械運作。本集團使用
been the major energy sources we used. Plants in Mainland China           的主要能源分別為柴油和煤，中國內地及
and Vietnam have their own coal-fired generators to ensure                越南廠房均自設燃煤發電機組以確保為高
availability of sufficient power necessary for the high power             耗電的造紙設備提供足夠電力，並沒有自
consuming paper-making machines, and therefore have not                   國家電網購買電力。
purchased electricity power from the national power grid.


Improving energy efficiency is one of the ways to effectively reduce      提升能源效率是有效降低能源耗量的方法

Cope with Climate Change                                                 氣候變化應對
Global warming is notably affecting the natural climate by increasing    全球變暖顯著影響自然氣候，使得極端天
the frequency and intensity of extreme weather and natural disasters     氣加劇，暴雨、洪水及乾旱等天災的頻
such as rainstorm, flood and drought, which greatly aggravated the       率和強度增加，大幅加劇了各類天災的破
damage and impact of various natural disasters. To cope with the         壞及影響程度。為應對越趨嚴重的氣候威
intensified threat of climate change, management of our Group has        脅，本集團的管理層定期評估生產經營中
regularly assessed the potential risks that may arise from cause         因氣候變化而可能導致的潛在風險，並採
climate change during production and operation and adopted               取預防及應變措施。
preventive and contingency measures.


We have strategically selected location for our plants and drawn up      我 們 策 略 性 地 為 廠 房 選 址 ， 制 定《 極 端 天
the “Extreme Weather Catastrophes Contingency Plan“. We classify         氣 災 害 應 急 預 案 》， 按 照 風 險 可 能 造 成 的
the extreme weather catastrophes into four degrees, namely very          危害程度，將極端天氣災害分為四個等
significant, significant, major and general according to the degree of   級：特別重大、重大、較大、一般。同
harm that may be caused by such risks. Meanwhile, we have                時，我們設立了清晰的應變體系，成立了
established a clear contingency system and set up the Steering           極端天氣應急救援領導小組，明文規定了
Committee on Extreme Weather Contingency and Rescue, which               各成員職能、預警行動和應急措施。我們
expressly stipulates functions of each member, alert actions and         亦會日常定期向員工進行宣傳、培訓和演
contingency measures. We arrange publicity, trainings and drills for     習，提高員工的警惕和應災能力，於颱

     history. Credit sales of products are made to customers with an          額。產品信貸銷售會提供予具有良
     appropriate credit history. In addition, the Group reviews the           好信貸紀錄之客戶。此外，本集團
     recoverable amount of debt at the end of the reporting period            會於報告期末檢討債項之可收回數
     to ensure that adequate impairment losses are recognised for             額，以確保就不可收回數額確認足
     irrecoverable amounts.                                                   夠之減值虧損。

4.   Liquidity risk                                                      4.   流動資金風險

     maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents deemed                    察及保持管理層視為足夠水平之
     adequate by management to finance the Group’s operations                 現金及現金等價物，以撥付本集團
     and mitigate the effects of fluctuations in cash flows. The              營運所需及減低現金流量波動之影
     management monitors the utilisation of bank borrowings and               響。管理層會監察銀行借貸之使用
     ensures compliance with the relevant loan covenants.                     情況，並確保遵守相關貸款契諾。


     Details of the liquidity risk are set out in note 36 to the              流動資金風險詳情載列於綜合財務

preparation of consolidated financial statements that are free from       導致的重大錯誤陳述。
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.


In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are     在編製綜合財務報表時，董事負責評
responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going      估   貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用情
concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern      況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及使
and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors      用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have      將   貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他
no realistic alternative but to do so.                                    實際的替代方案。

2. APPLICATION OF NEW AND AMENDMENTS                                     2.   應用新訂及經修訂香港財務
   TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                           報告準則（「香港財務報告準
   STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (CONTINUED)                                           則」（
                                                                                ）續）

      Impacts on application of Amendments to                                 應用香港會計準則第8號（修訂本）
      HKAS 8 Definition of Accounting Estimates                               會計估計之定義 之影響

      The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the          本集團於本年度首次採納該修訂
      current year. The amendments define accounting estimates as             本。該等修訂將會計估計定義為「存
      “monetary amounts in financial statements that are subject to           在計量不明朗因素的財務報表之貨
      measurement uncertainty”. An accounting policy may require              幣金額」。會計政策可能規定對涉及
      items in financial statements to be measured in a way that              計量不明朗因素的財務報表的項目
      involves measurement uncertainty. In such a case, an entity             進行計量。於此情況下，一間實體
      develops an accounting estimate to achieve the objective set            應編製會計估計，旨在達到會計政
      out by the accounting policy. The amendments to HKAS 8                  策載列的目標。香港會計準則第8號
      clarify the distinction between changes in accounting estimates,        之修訂澄清了會計估計變更與會計
      and changes in accounting policies and the correction of errors.        政策變更及錯誤更正之間的區別。


      The application of the amendments in the current year had no            本年度應用該等修訂對本集團之綜
      material impact on the consolidated financial statements.               合財務報表並無重大影響。


      Impacts on application of Amendments to                                 應用香港會計準則第1號及香港財
      HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice Statement 2                                   務報告準則實務報告第2號（修訂
      Disclosure of Accounting Policies                                        會計政策之披露 之影響
                                                                              本）

      The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the          本集團於本年度期間已首次採納該
      current year. HKAS 1 Presentation of Financial Statements is            等 修 訂 本 。 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 1 號財
      amended to replace all instances of the term “significant               務報表列報 修訂以「重大會計政策資
      accounting policies” with “material accounting policy                   料」取代「主要會計政策」一詞的所有
      information”. Accounting policy information is material if,             實例。倘連同實體財務報表內其他
      when considered together with other information included in             資料一併考慮，會計政策資料可以
      an entity’s financial statements, it can reasonably be expected         合理預期會影響通用財務報表的主
      to influence decisions that the primary users of general purpose        要使用者根據該等財務報表所作出
      financial statements make on the basis of those financial               的決定，則該會計政策資料屬重大。
      statements.

     The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information          該等修訂亦澄清，即使涉及款項並
     may be material because of the nature of the related                    不重大，但基於相關交易性質、其
     transactions, other events or conditions, even if the amounts           他事項或情況，會計政策資料仍可
     are immaterial. However, not all accounting policy information          屬重大。然而，並非所有與重大交
     relating to material transactions, other events or conditions is        易、其他事項或情況有關的會計政
     itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose immaterial            策資料本身即屬重大。倘一間實體
     accounting policy information, such information must not                選擇披露非重大會計政策資料，有
     obscure material accounting policy information.                         關資料不得掩蓋重大會計政策資料。


     HKFRS Practice Statement 2 Making Materiality Judgements                香港財務報告準則實務報告第2號作
     (the “Practice Statement”) is also amended to illustrate how an         出 重 大 性 判 斷（「 實 務 報 告 」）亦 經 修
     entity applies the “four-step materiality process” to accounting        訂，以說明一間實體如何將「四步法
     policy disclosures and to judge whether information about an            評估重大性流程」應用於會計政策披
     accounting policy is material to its financial statements.              露及判斷有關一項會計政策的資料
     Guidance and examples are added to the Practice Statement.              對其財務報表是否屬重大。實務報
                                                                             告已增加指導意見及實例。


     The application of the amendments has had no material impact            於本年度應用該等修訂對本集團財
     on the Group’s financial positions and performance but has              務狀況及表現無重大影響，但可能
     affected the disclosure of the Group’s accounting policies to           影響本集團於綜合財務報表中的會
     the consolidated financial statements.                                  計政策披露。

          over time by reference to the progress towards complete                度，當達至下面其中一項時，
          satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one             控制權隨時間的推移而轉移及
          of the following criteria is met:                                      收入隨時間的推移而確認：


          •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                •    客戶同時接收及耗用由本
                the benefits provided by the Group’s performance                      集團執行本集團履約所提
                as the Group performs;                                                供的效益；


          •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an                   •    本集團的履約產生及提升
                asset that the customer controls as the Group                         一項資產，而該項資產於
                performs; or                                                          本集團履約時由客戶控
                                                                                      制；或


          •     the Group’s performance does not create an asset                 •    本集團的履約不會產生對
                with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                    本集團有其他用途的資產
                has an enforceable right to payment for                               及本集團對迄今完成的履
                performance completed to date.                                        約付款有可執行之權利。


          Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when               否則，當客戶取得不同貨品的
          the customer obtains control of the distinct good.                     控制權時按一個時間點確認收
                                                                                 入。


          A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to              合約負債指本集團已從客戶收
          transfer goods to a customer for which the Group has                   取代價（或代價金額已到期）而
          received consideration (or an amount of consideration is               本集團有責任去轉移貨品予客
          due) from the customer.                                                戶。


          Revenue from sale of packaging paper, pulp and tissue                  來自銷售包裝紙、木漿及衛生
          paper is recognised at a point in time when the customer               紙之收入於客戶取得貨品的控
          obtains control of goods.                                              制權時按一個時間點確認。

          Leases                                                               租賃
          Definition of a lease                                                租賃之定義
          A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys          當合約傳達權利以控制使用一
          the right to control the use of an identified asset for a            項已識別資產一段期間以換取
          period of time in exchange for consideration.                        代價，則該合約乃（或包含）一
                                                                               項租賃。


          For contracts entered into or modified on or after the               於 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號
          date of initial application of HKFRS 16 Lease or arising             租賃 初始應用日期或之後訂立
          from business combinations, the Group assesses whether               或修改或由業務合併引起之合
          a contract is or contains a lease based on the definition            約，本集團根據香港財務報告
          under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or                    準則第16號之定義於初始時、
          acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be          修改日期或收購日期，倘適
          reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the                    用 ， 評 估 一 份 合 約 是 否（ 或 包
          contract are subsequently changed.                                   含 ）一 項 租 賃 。 除 非 合 約 之 條
                                                                               款及條件其後改變，此合約將
                                                                               不會再評估。

          from the commencement date and do not contain a                      買權之租賃應用短期租賃確認

          are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or                租約期內按直線法或其他系統
          another systematic basis over the lease term.                        基準確認為開支。

          Foreign currencies                                                    外幣
          In preparing the financial statements of each individual              於編製各個別集團實體之財務
          group entity, transactions in currencies other than the               報表時，以該實體功能貨幣以

          currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that           率重新換算。非貨幣項目按外

          historical cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.           算。

          the retranslation of monetary items, and on the                       生之匯兌差額於其產生期間於
          retranslation of monetary items, are recognised in profit             損益內確認，惟源自其結算並

          or payable to a foreign operation for which settlement is             匯 兌 差 額（ 因 此 構 成 境 外 業 務
          neither planned nor likely to occur (therefore forming                淨 投 資 之 一 部 分 ）除 外 ， 該 差
          part of the net investment in the foreign operation),                 額初始於其他全面收益表確

          income.

          operations are translated into the presentation currency              採用各報告期末當日之匯率換
          of the Group (i.e. HK$) using exchange rates prevailing at            算 為 本 集 團 之 列 賬 貨 幣（ 即 港
          the end of each reporting period. Income and expenses                 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支 出 項 目 乃 按
          items are translated at the average exchange rates for the            該期間之平均匯率進行換算，
          period, unless exchange rates fluctuate significantly                 除非匯率於該期間內出現大幅

          the date of transactions are used. Exchange differences               則採用於交易當日之匯率。所

          Borrowing costs                                                       借貸成本
          Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,             與購置、建造或生產合資格資
          construction or production of qualifying assets, which are            產（ 需 一 段 長 時 間 方 可 作 擬 定
          assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to          用 途 或 可 供 銷 售 的 資 產 ）直 接
          get ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the            有關之借貸成本均加至該等資
          cost of those assets until such time as the assets are                產之成本，直至該等資產大致
          substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                   可作其擬定用途或可供銷售為
                                                                                止。

          included in the general borrowing pool for calculation of             貸計入一般借貸池，以計算一

          All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss            所有其他借貸成本均於產生期

          Government grants are not recognised until there is                   政府補助於合理保證本集團將

          conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be              補助的情況下方會予以確認。
          received.


          Government grants related to income that are receivable               與收入有關的政府補助，乃對
          as compensation for expenses or losses already incurred               已產生的費用或虧損，或向本
          or for the purpose of giving immediate financial support              集團提供即時的財政支持而
          to the Group with no future related costs are recognised              並無未來的相關成本之應收補
          in profit or loss in the period in which they become                  償，於變為應收期間在損益中
          receivable. Such grants are presented under “other                    確 認 。 有 關 補 助 呈 列 於「 其 他
          income”.                                                              收入」項下。

          Taxation                                                               稅項
          Income tax expense represents the sum of current and                   所得稅支出指本期及遞延所得
          deferred income tax expense.                                           稅支出的總和。


          The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for               現時應付稅項乃按年度應課稅
          the year. Taxable profit differs from ‘profit before tax’              盈利計算。應課稅盈利與除稅
          because of items of income or expense that are taxable                 前盈利不同乃源於其他年度應
          or deductible in other years and items that are never                  課稅或可扣稅的收入或開支項
          taxable or deductible. The Group’s current tax is                      目及從未課稅及扣稅的項目。
          calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                   本集團之本期稅項乃按報告期
          substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.              末已實行或大致上已實行之稅
                                                                                 率計算。


          Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                    遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表
          between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in              之資產及負債賬面值與計算應
          the consolidated financial statements and the                          課稅盈利所採用相應稅基的暫
          corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                     時差額而確認。遞延稅項負債
          taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                 一般就所有應課稅暫時差額予
          recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred             以確認。遞延稅項資產一般就
          tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                 所有可扣稅暫時差額確認，惟
          temporary differences to the extent that it is probable                僅於可能有應課稅盈利將可以
          that taxable profits will be available against which those             使用該可扣稅暫時差額時確
          deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                 認。倘暫時差額乃由初始確認
          deferred assets and liabilities are not recognised if the              一項不影響應課稅盈利或會計
          temporary difference arises from the initial recognition               盈 利 的 交 易 的 資 產 及 負 債（ 於
          (other than in a business combination) of assets and                   業 務 合 併 中 除 外 ）所 引 起 ， 且
          liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable          交易時不會產生相等的應課稅
          profit nor the accounting profit and at the time of the                及可扣稅暫時差額，則有關遞
          transaction does not give rise to equal taxable and                    延資產及負債不予確認。
          deductible temporary differences.

          Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable                       遞延稅項負債一般就與附屬公
          temporary differences associated with investments in                      司之投資而引致之應課稅暫時
          subsidiaries, except where the Group is able to control                   差額而確認，惟若本集團可控
          the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable               制暫時差額之撥回及暫時差額
          that the temporary difference will not reverse in the                     有可能不會於可見將來撥回之
          foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from                      情況則除外。與該等投資及權
          deductible temporary differences associated with such                     益相關的可扣稅暫時差額所產
          investments and interest are only recognised to the                       生的遞延稅項資產，僅在可能
          extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient                  有足夠應課稅盈利可以使用暫
          taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of the              時差額之效益且預計於可見將
          temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in                 來可以撥回時確認。
          the foreseeable future.


          The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                 遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報
          the end of the reporting period and reduced to the                        告期末作檢討，並在沒可能會
          extent that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable              有足夠應課稅盈利以收回全部
          profits will be available to allow all or part of the asset to            或部分資產時加以遞減。
          be recovered.


          Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax               遞延稅項資產及負債按預期負
          rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the               債清償或資產變現期間內應用
          liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate          之稅率，以報告期末已生效或
          (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                    實際上已生效之稅率（及稅法）
          enacted by the end of the reporting period.                               為基準計量。


          The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                    遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反
          reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                  映本集團於報告期末所預期對
          manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                      收回或償還其資產及負債之
          reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount                賬面值之方式所產生的稅務後
          of its assets and liabilities.                                            果。

          amount, but so that the increased carrying amount does              回金額，惟增加之賬面值不得

          determined had no impairment loss been recognised for               於過往年度並無確認任何減值
          the asset (or a cash-generating unit) in prior years. A             虧損所釐定之賬面值。撥回之
          reversal of an impairment loss is recognised immediately            減值虧損即時在損益中確認。
          in profit or loss.

          (b)   cash equivalents, which comprises of short-term                (b)   現金等價物，包括短期
                (generally with original maturity of three months or                 （一般為原到期日為三個
                less), highly liquid investments that are readily                    月 或 以 下 ）、 可 隨 時 轉 換
                convertible to a known amount of cash and which                      為已知金額現金且價值變
                are subject to an insignificant risk of changes in                   動風險不大之高流動性投
                value. Cash equivalents are held for the purpose of                  資。現金等價物乃為滿足

                for investment or other purposes.                                    非為投資或其他目的持

          Impairment of financial assets                                          金融資產減值
          The Group performs impairment assessment under                          本集團於金融資產就預期信貸
          expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                  虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進
          which are subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS                  行減值評估乃視乎香港財務準
          9 (including trade and other receivables, loan to a joint               則第9號項下的減值評估（包括
          venture, amounts due from related companies and bank                    應收貿易及其他賬款、合營企
          balances). The amount of ECL is updated at each                         業貸款、應收關連公司款項及
          reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial          銀 行 結 餘 ）。 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 金
          recognition.                                                            額於每個報告日期更新以反映
                                                                                  自初始確認以來之信貸風險變
                                                                                  動。


          Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all               終身預期信貸虧損指由所有可
          possible default events over the expected life of the                   能違約事件超出相關工具預期
          relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                    壽命將引致之預期信貸虧損。
          ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                    相 反 ， 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損
          expected to result from default events that are possible                （「 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 」）指
          within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment is                終身預期信貸虧損預期從可能
          done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                        於報告日期後12個月內的違約
          experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               事件引致之部分。所作評估基
          debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  於本集團過往信貸虧損記錄，
          of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                 並調整債務人的特定因素，普
          well as the forecast of future conditions.                              遍經濟狀況及同時對於報告日
                                                                                  期之現況及預測未來狀況之評
                                                                                  估。

          The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                      本集團通常就應收貿易賬款確
          receivables.                                                            認終身預期信貸虧損。


          For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                  就所有其他工具，本集團以相
          allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a                     等 於 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 計
          significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          量虧損撥備，除非自初始確認
          in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                    後信貸風險顯著增加，本集團
          assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                 則確認終身預期信貸虧損。評
          is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk             估是否應確認終身預期信貸虧
          of a default occurring since initial recognition.                       損乃基於自初始確認後發生違
                                                                                  約的可能性或風險是否顯著增
                                                                                  加。


          (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                               (i)   信貸風險顯著增加


                In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                      於評估自初始確認後信貸
                significantly since initial recognition, the Group                      風險是否顯著增加時，本
                compares the risk of a default occurring on the                         集團將於報告日期金融工
                financial instrument as at the reporting date with                      具發生違約之風險與於初
                the risk of a default occurring on the financial                        始確認日期金融工具發生
                instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In                    違約之風險作比較。作出
                making this assessment, the Group considers both                        評估時，本集團同時考慮
                quantitative and qualitative information that is                        合理及有理據的定量及定
                reasonable and supportable, including historical                        性資料，包括過往經驗及
                experience and forward-looking information that is                      無需花費成本或精力而可
                available without undue cost or effort.                                 得之前瞻性資料。

                 the Group).                                                              團認為發生違約事件。

          (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                               (iii)   信貸減值金融資產


                  A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or                       當一項或多項違約事件發
                  more events of default that have a detrimental                         生令金融資產估計未來現
                  impact on the estimated future cash flows of that                      金流量具不利影響，該金
                  financial asset have occurred.                                         融資產則信貸減值。


          (iv)    Write-off policy                                               (iv)    撇銷政策


                  The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                   當有資料顯示對手方存在
                  information indicating that the counterparty is in                     嚴重財政困難及並無實際
                  severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                  把握收回款項，例如，當
                  prospect of recovery, for example, when the                            對手方已進行清盤或已
                  counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                      進入破產程序，或如為
                  has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, or in the                     應收貿易賬款，當金額乃
                  case of trade receivables, when the amounts are                        逾期超過兩年，以較早者
                  over two years past due, whichever occurs sooner.                      為準，本集團撇銷金融資
                  Financial assets written off may still be subject to                   產。金融資產的撇銷可能
                  enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                      仍須根據本集團回收程序
                  procedures, taking into account legal advice where                     的執行方式，當適用時考
                  appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                   慮法律建議。撇銷構成剔
                  event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in                     除確認事項。任何其後回
                  profit or loss.                                                        撥於損益確認。

          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確
                                                                                      認


                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                           預期信貸虧損計量具違約
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                  可 能 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                  約 的 損 失 幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝
                exposure at default. The assessment of the                            光的功能。違約可能性及
                probability of default and loss given default is based                默認虧損的評估乃基於過
                on historical data and forward-looking information.                   往數據及前瞻性資料。預
                Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                            期信貸虧損的估計反映不
                probability-weighted amount that is determined                        偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
                with the respective risks of default occurring as the                 發生違約相關風險的比重
                weights.                                                              來釐定。


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                      一般而言，預期信貸虧損
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                   為根據合約所有應付本集
                accordance with the contract and the cash flows                       團合約現金流量與本集團
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                      於初始確認時預期收到，
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                     按實際利率折現的現金流
                recognition.                                                          量的差額。

4. CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                    4.   關鍵會計判斷及估計不明朗
   KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                                      因素之主要來源（續）
     (CONTINUED)

     Key sources of estimation uncertainty (Continued)                        估計不明朗因素之主要來源
                                                                                         （續）

     Provision of ECL for trade receivables                                   應收貿易賬款預期信貸虧損撥備
     The Group uses collective assessment through grouping of                 本集團於考慮貿易債務人之內部信
     various debtors that have similar loss patterns after considering        貸評級、還款賬齡記錄及╱或各應
     internal credit ratings of trade debtor, aging repayment history         收貿易賬款之逾期狀況後，將具有
     and/or past due status of respective trade receivables, to               類似虧損模式的不同債務人組合作
     calculate ECL for the trade receivables (excluding those backed          集體評估，以計算應收貿易賬款（不
     by bills). In calculating the ECL, the loss rates are estimated          包 括 有 票 據 質 押 者 ）的 預 期 信 貸 虧
     based on comparable probability of default by internal credit            損。於計算預期信貸虧損時，損失
     ratings through groupings of various debtors that have similar           率根據內部信用評級的可比較違約
     loss patterns and recovery rate quoted from international                概率進行估算，而可比較違約概率
     credit-rating agencies and adjusted for forward-looking                  乃通過將具備類似虧損模式及追償
     information. Such calculation of ECL has involved subjective             率（ 國 際 信 用 評 級 機 構 所 引 用 ）之 不
     judgment and management estimates. At every reporting                    同債務人進行分組，並就前瞻性資
     dates, the loss rates are reassessed and changes in the forward-         料作調整而得出。就此計算預期信
     looking information are considered. In addition, credit-impaired         貸虧損涉及主觀判斷及管理層的估
     trade receivables (excluding those backed by bills) and trade            計。於每一報告日期，會再次評估
     receivables backed by bills are assessed for ECL individually.           虧損率及考慮前瞻性資料的變化。
                                                                              此外，信貸減值之應收貿易賬款（不
                                                                              包括有票據質押者）及有票據質押之
                                                                              應收貿易賬款會被個別評估預期信
                                                                              貸虧損。


     The provision of ECL is sensitive to changes in estimates. The           預期信貸虧損撥備對估計的變化相
     information about the ECL and the Group’s trade receivables              當敏感。有關預期信貸虧損及本集

     For both years, the Group entered into leases for certain of its     於兩個年度，本集團就若干員工宿

     negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range of        條款乃按個別基準磋商，並包含廣
     different terms and conditions. In determining the lease term        泛的不同條款及條件。於釐定租期
     and assessing the length of the non-cancellable period, the          及評估不可撤銷期限年期時，本集
     Group applies the definition of a contract and determines the        團應用合約之定義及釐定可行使合
     period for which the contract is enforceable.                        約之期間。


     In addition, the Group owns several industrial buildings where       此外，本集團擁有數個其主要生產
     its manufacturing facilities are primarily located and office        設施所在之工業樓宇及辦公室樓
     buildings. The Group is the registered owner of these property       宇。本集團乃此等物業權益（包括相
     interests, including the underlying leasehold lands. Lump sum        關租賃土地）之登記持有人。購入此
     payments were made upfront to acquire these property                 等物業權益時作出一次性預付款。
     interests.

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                     19. 投資物業

      The Group leases out commercial and industrial properties                 本集團根據經營租賃按每月付租出
      under operating leases with rentals payable monthly. The leases           租商業及工業物業。租賃通常初始
      typically run for an initial period of 1 to 10 years, with unilateral     年期為1至10年，承租人單方面有權
      rights to extend the lease beyond initial period held by lessees          延續租賃至初始年期外。大部分租
      only. Majority of the lease contracts contain annual lease                賃合約在租期內固定年度租賃付款。
      payment that are fixed over the lease term.


      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of          本集團在租賃安排並無承受外幣風
      the lease arrangements, as all leases are denominated in the              險，因所有租賃乃按集團實體相關
      respective functional currencies of group entities. The lease             功能貨幣計值。租賃合約並無包含
      contracts do not contain residual value guarantee and lessee’s            剩餘價值保證及承租人於租期完結
      option to purchase the property at the end of lease term.                 時購入物業之權利。

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               35. 資本風險管理

      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the          本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
      Group will be able to continue as a going concern while               實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
      maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation        債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
      of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy          最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
      remains unchanged from prior year.                                    與去年一致。


      The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which           本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
      include the bank borrowings and lease liabilities as disclosed in     包括分別於附註30及28披露之銀行
      notes 30 and 28 respectively, and equity attributable to equity       借貸及租賃負債，以及本公司權益
      holders of the Company, comprising issued share capital,              持有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、
      various reserves and perpetual capital securities.                    多項儲備及永續資本證券）。


      The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a        本公司董事不時檢討資本結構。作
      regular basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the     為檢討的一部分，董事考慮資本成
      cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of           本以及與各類資本有關的風險。根
      capital. Based on recommendations of the directors, the Group         據董事的建議，本集團將通過派付
